# L5-06 — LANE 5 ATOMIC TASK LIST
# Lane 5 — Access, Infra and Ops. Subsystems K, L, M, Q, R (spec §99.2).
# Conforms to `Code/implementation/PARTITION.md` v1 (FROZEN). Nothing here overrides it.

---

## 1. How to use this document

You are the executor. Work **top to bottom**. Do not skip a task whose dependencies are unmet.
Every task below is either:

| Mode | Who executes | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| **LANE** | you (Sonnet-4.6-class AI developer) | Fully specified. No design, no choosing, no interpreting. Run the commands. |
| **ASSISTED** | **L0 human lead**, after you prepare | Requires a credential, a console session, hardware, money or a legal identity that a low-cost agent must not hold. You produce the preparation artifact and the evidence template only, then **stop and hand off**. |

**You never execute an ASSISTED task's console steps.** You produce its artifact, commit it, and open the handoff issue. If you find yourself about to type a token, a password, a card number, an org-settings URL or an SSH session into a production host — stop; it is ASSISTED.

**Repository:** `control-plane` (per PARTITION Repositories table).
**Branch prefix:** `lane/5/*`. **Merge target:** `integration`. **Merge-train position:** last (L1 → L4 → L2 → L3 → **L5**).

### 1.1 Paths Lane 5 owns (exclusively)

```
access/**      infra/**      ops-vm/**      notify/**      assets/**
```

### 1.2 Paths Lane 5 must NEVER write (lane-guard CI will fail the PR)

| Path | Owner |
|---|---|
| `schemas/registry/**`, `schemas/product/**`, `registries/**`, `validators/registry/**` | L1 |
| `.github/workflows/**`, `templates/workflows/**`, `tools/evidence/**` | L2 |
| `reconciler/**`, `tools/provision/**`, `validators/drift/**` | L3 |
| `schemas/records/**`, `metrics/**`, `tools/records/**`, all of `control-plane-records` | L4 |
| `contracts/**`, `CODEOWNERS`, `docs/**`, root files, `Makefile` | L0 |

Three consequences you must not work around:

1. **Lane 5 writes no GitHub Actions workflow.** Every scheduled job in this lane ships as a CLI entrypoint inside an owned path. The workflow that calls it is requested from L2 via **L5-T58**.
2. **Lane 5 writes no file under `docs/`.** `docs/control-plane-rebuild.md` (spec §45.4) is an L0 file. Lane 5 produces `ops-vm/rebuild/runbook.src.md` and hands it to L0 (**L5-T22**).
3. **Lane 5 writes nothing into `records/**` or `events/**`.** Tools that produce a record emit a payload to a `--out` path; L4's write path commits it. Never `git clone` the records repository.

### 1.3 Out of Lane 5 scope — do not build, do not stub

Subsystem **J** (Background Machine Layer, spec §37) is **not assigned to any build lane** by PARTITION. Consequently **AT-108** (ops console read-only) and **AT-109** (background cage egress wall) are **out of Lane 5 scope** and are routed to L0. Where a spec paragraph in §35/§36/§49/§62 describes a Hermes host, Lane 5 builds only the parts that live in `access/**`, `assets/**` or `ops-vm/**` — the approved-list enforcement, the asset-inventory entry and the ops-VM separation assertion — and never the harness, the cage, the egress override or the systemd stop unit.

## 2. Preconditions (run once, before L5-T01)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
# Set these two literals for your machine, then paste the block.
export REPO_ROOT="$HOME/work/control-plane"          # checkout of the control-plane repo
export SPEC="C:/D_Drive/PS/MultiProduct/Research/MultiProduct_MasterSpec_v4.0.md"   # the frozen spec, read-only
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git rev-parse --show-toplevel
python3 -c "import sys; sys.exit(0 if sys.version_info[:2] == (3, 12) else 1)" \
  || { echo "STOP: Python 3.12 required (FD-005); got $(python3 --version 2>&1)"; exit 1; }
test -f "$SPEC" && echo "SPEC OK" || echo "SPEC MISSING"
git fetch origin && git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
echo "PRECONDITIONS PASS"
```

Expected output ends with:

```
SPEC OK
PRECONDITIONS PASS
```

If `SPEC MISSING` prints, **STOP** — open a blocker (§3) with `TASK=preconditions`. Do not guess spec content from memory.

**Python interpreter for the whole lane** (set in every shell before running lane tools; created by L5-T02):

```bash
set -euo pipefail
if [ -x "$HOME/.venv-l5/bin/python" ]; then export L5PY="$HOME/.venv-l5/bin/python";
else export L5PY="$HOME/.venv-l5/Scripts/python.exe"; fi
"$L5PY" --version
```

---

## 3. STOP rule and the blocker-issue template

**The STOP rule, stated once and binding on every task:** if a task's commands fail in a way its own STOP clause names, or if any step would require you to choose between two options the task does not name, or if a file you must read does not exist — **do not proceed, do not improvise, do not stub**. Commit nothing. Open a blocker issue from this exact template and move to the next task whose dependencies are already met.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
gh issue create \
  --repo "$(gh repo view --json nameWithOwner -q .nameWithOwner)" \
  --title "BLOCKER L5 <TASK_ID>: <one-line symptom>" \
  --label "blocker,lane-5" \
  --body "$(cat <<'EOF'
TASK: <TASK_ID>
PHASE: <P0..P7>
MODE: <LANE|ASSISTED>
SPEC REFERENCE: <§ number cited by the task>
DEPENDS ON: <task ids>

WHAT I RAN (verbatim):
```
<paste the exact commands>
```

WHAT I EXPECTED (from the task's SELF-VERIFY block):
```
<paste the expected output>
```

WHAT I GOT (verbatim):
```
<paste the actual output, unedited>
```

WHICH STOP CLAUSE FIRED:
<quote the task's STOP line>

DECISION REQUIRED FROM L0 (I am not authorised to make it):
<state the choice, and the options as the task names them; if the task names no options, write "none named">

BLOCKED TASKS DOWNSTREAM: <task ids that depend on this one>
EOF
)"
```

## 5. TASK DETAIL — PHASE P0, LANE BOOTSTRAP

### L5-T01 — Lane tree and ownership manifest
**Phase** P0 · **Size** S · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** — · **Spec** PARTITION §"The five build lanes"
**Files:** `access/.gitkeep`, `infra/.gitkeep`, `ops-vm/.gitkeep`, `notify/.gitkeep`, `assets/.gitkeep`, `infra/OWNERSHIP.md`, `infra/tools/lane_tree_check.sh` (all new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git fetch origin && git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p0-t01-lane-tree
mkdir -p access/model access/tools access/tests access/schema access/assisted access/invariants access/acceptance
mkdir -p access/ai-runtime/tools access/ai-runtime/benchmark access/layerb/tools access/layerb/instance access/layerb/selfview access/layerb/backup access/layerb/restore access/layerb/assisted
mkdir -p infra/tools infra/handoff
mkdir -p ops-vm/compose ops-vm/provision ops-vm/tools ops-vm/tests ops-vm/slo ops-vm/patching ops-vm/offvm ops-vm/rebuild ops-vm/assisted
mkdir -p notify/contract notify/routing notify/tools notify/tests notify/escalation notify/assisted
mkdir -p assets/schema assets/inventory assets/tools assets/tests assets/deadlines
for d in access infra ops-vm notify assets; do touch "$d/.gitkeep"; done
printf '%s\n' \
 '# Lane 5 ownership manifest' \
 'Lane 5 (Access, Infra and Ops) owns exactly these paths in control-plane:' \
 'access/**  infra/**  ops-vm/**  notify/**  assets/**' \
 'Subsystems: K (AI runtime contract enforcement), L (access-control architecture),' \
 'M (operations VM), Q (asset inventory and deadline watch), R (notification routing).' \
 'Lane 5 writes NO file under: contracts/, docs/, CODEOWNERS, Makefile, root files,' \
 'schemas/, registries/, validators/, .github/workflows/, templates/, tools/,' \
 'reconciler/, metrics/, and nothing in the control-plane-records repository.' \
 'Source of this rule: Code/implementation/PARTITION.md v1 (FROZEN).' > infra/OWNERSHIP.md
printf '%s\n' \
 '#!/usr/bin/env bash' 'set -euo pipefail' 'missing=0' \
 'for d in access infra ops-vm notify assets; do [ -d "$d" ] || { echo "MISSING DIR: $d"; missing=1; }; done' \
 '[ -f infra/OWNERSHIP.md ] || { echo "MISSING FILE: infra/OWNERSHIP.md"; missing=1; }' \
 '[ "$missing" -eq 0 ] && echo "L5-T01 SELF-VERIFY PASS" || exit 1' > infra/tools/lane_tree_check.sh
chmod +x infra/tools/lane_tree_check.sh
git add access infra ops-vm notify assets
git commit -m "L5-T01: lane 5 directory tree and ownership manifest"
git push -u origin lane/5/p0-t01-lane-tree
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. The five owned top-level directories exist and are tracked (`git ls-files access infra ops-vm notify assets` returns ≥ 6 paths).
2. `infra/OWNERSHIP.md` names all five owned paths and lists the forbidden paths.
3. `git diff --name-only integration...HEAD` contains no path outside the five owned roots.

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
bash infra/tools/lane_tree_check.sh
git diff --name-only integration...HEAD | grep -vqE '^(access|infra|ops-vm|notify|assets)/' && echo "PATH GUARD FAIL" || echo "PATH GUARD PASS"
```
Expected output (exact, two lines):
```
L5-T01 SELF-VERIFY PASS
PATH GUARD PASS
```
**STOP** if any of the five directories already contains tracked content authored by another lane, or if `PATH GUARD FAIL` prints. Open a blocker (§3) with `TASK=L5-T01`.

---

### L5-T02 — Python toolchain pin and venv bootstrap
**Phase** P0 · **Size** S · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T01
**Files:** `infra/requirements.txt`, `infra/tools/bootstrap_venv.sh` (new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p0-t02-toolchain
printf '%s\n' 'PyYAML==6.0.2' 'jsonschema==4.23.0' 'pytest==8.3.3' 'requests==2.32.3' 'cryptography==43.0.1' > infra/requirements.txt
printf '%s\n' \
 '#!/usr/bin/env bash' 'set -euo pipefail' 'VENV="$HOME/.venv-l5"' \
 'python -m venv "$VENV" 2>/dev/null || python3 -m venv "$VENV"' \
 'if [ -x "$VENV/bin/python" ]; then PY="$VENV/bin/python"; else PY="$VENV/Scripts/python.exe"; fi' \
 '"$PY" -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -q -r infra/requirements.txt' \
 'echo "L5PY=$PY"' > infra/tools/bootstrap_venv.sh
chmod +x infra/tools/bootstrap_venv.sh
bash infra/tools/bootstrap_venv.sh
if [ -x "$HOME/.venv-l5/bin/python" ]; then export L5PY="$HOME/.venv-l5/bin/python"; else export L5PY="$HOME/.venv-l5/Scripts/python.exe"; fi
git add infra/requirements.txt infra/tools/bootstrap_venv.sh
git commit -m "L5-T02: pin lane 5 python toolchain"
git push -u origin lane/5/p0-t02-toolchain
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. `infra/requirements.txt` pins every dependency with `==`; no ranges, no unpinned names (invariant 85 discipline applied to this lane's own tooling).
2. The venv exists and imports all five packages.
3. The venv lives outside `$REPO_ROOT`, so nothing untracked enters the repository.

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
grep -cE '^[A-Za-z].*==[0-9]' infra/requirements.txt
"$L5PY" -c "import yaml,jsonschema,pytest,requests,cryptography;print('L5-T02 SELF-VERIFY PASS')"
case "$HOME/.venv-l5" in "$REPO_ROOT"*) echo "VENV INSIDE REPO";; *) echo "VENV OUTSIDE REPO";; esac
```
Expected output (exact, three lines):
```
5
L5-T02 SELF-VERIFY PASS
VENV OUTSIDE REPO
```
**STOP** if `pip install` fails for any pinned version. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T02`; never relax or float a pin to make the install succeed.

### L5-T03 — Lane path-guard self-check
**Phase** P0 · **Size** S · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T01 · **Spec** PARTITION rule 1
**Files:** `infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh` (new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p0-t03-lane-guard
printf '%s\n' \
 '#!/usr/bin/env bash' \
 '# Usage: bash infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh <base-ref>' \
 'set -euo pipefail' \
 'BASE="${1:-integration}"' \
 "ALLOW='^(access|infra|ops-vm|notify|assets)/'" \
 'BAD="$(git diff --name-only "$BASE"...HEAD | grep -vE "$ALLOW" || true)"' \
 'if [ -n "$BAD" ]; then echo "LANE GUARD FAIL - foreign paths touched:"; echo "$BAD"; exit 1; fi' \
 'echo "LANE GUARD PASS"' > infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh
chmod +x infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh
git add infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh
git commit -m "L5-T03: lane path-guard self-check"
git push -u origin lane/5/p0-t03-lane-guard
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. The script exits `0` and prints `LANE GUARD PASS` when only owned paths changed.
2. The script exits `1` and lists offenders when a foreign path is present in the diff.
3. The allowlist regex names exactly the five owned roots and nothing else.

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
bash infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh integration
grep -c "access|infra|ops-vm|notify|assets" infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh
```
Expected output (exact, two lines):
```
LANE GUARD PASS
1
```
**STOP** if the guard reports foreign paths on a branch you authored: revert those files. Never widen the allowlist. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T03`.

### L5-T04 — Contract-input inventory (read-only inputs)
**Phase** P0 · **Size** S · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T02 · **Spec** PARTITION rules 2 and 4
**Files:** `infra/contract-inputs.yaml`, `infra/tools/check_contract_inputs.py` (new)

Lane 5 consumes other lanes only through `contracts/**`. This task records exactly which contract files Lane 5 reads and fails closed when one is absent, so a missing contract is a blocker at hour one rather than a wrong assumption at week six.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p0-t04-contract-inputs
cat > infra/contract-inputs.yaml <<'YAML'
# Read-only inputs Lane 5 consumes from contracts/ (PARTITION rules 2 and 4).
# Lane 5 NEVER writes any of these. A missing entry is a blocker, never a stub.
inputs:
  - id: people-registry
    path: contracts/people.schema.json
    consumed_by: [L5-T14, L5-T49, L5-T52]
    reason: "capability holders for people-intelligence (spec 90.4)"
  - id: platform-event-enum
    path: contracts/platform.event-types.json
    consumed_by: [L5-T39, L5-T41]
    reason: "closed event_type enum; a push event absent from it cannot page anyone (spec 92.11, 97.3)"
  - id: product-contract
    path: contracts/product.schema.json
    consumed_by: [L5-T29, L5-T30, L5-T40]
    reason: "ai_restrictions, ai_runtime_dependency, alert_channel (spec 36.4, 38.3, 22)"
  - id: drift-finding
    path: contracts/drift-finding.schema.json
    consumed_by: [L5-T36, L5-T38, L5-T49]
    reason: "Lane 5 emits findings the L3 reconciler consumes (spec 53.1)"
  - id: record-envelope
    path: contracts/record-envelope.schema.json
    consumed_by: [L5-T30, L5-T50, L5-T54]
    reason: "payload shape L4 commits into records/ (spec 97.2)"
YAML
cat > infra/tools/check_contract_inputs.py <<'PY'
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Fail closed when a declared contract input is missing. Lane 5 never stubs a contract."""
import sys, pathlib, yaml
root = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
doc = yaml.safe_load((root / "infra" / "contract-inputs.yaml").read_text())
missing = [i["path"] for i in doc["inputs"] if not (root / i["path"]).exists()]
if missing:
    print("CONTRACT INPUTS MISSING:")
    for m in missing:
        print("  " + m)
    sys.exit(1)
print("L5-T04 SELF-VERIFY PASS: %d contract inputs present" % len(doc["inputs"]))
PY
git add infra/contract-inputs.yaml infra/tools/check_contract_inputs.py
git commit -m "L5-T04: declare read-only contract inputs for lane 5"
git push -u origin lane/5/p0-t04-contract-inputs
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. Every entry has `id`, `path`, non-empty `consumed_by` and `reason`.
2. Every `path` begins with `contracts/`.
3. The checker exits non-zero and names each absent file when any input is missing.

**SELF-VERIFY**

"$L5PY" -c "import yaml;d=yaml.safe_load(open('infra/contract-inputs.yaml'));assert all(i['path'].startswith('contracts/') and i['consumed_by'] and i['reason'] for i in d['inputs']);print('SHAPE OK',len(d['inputs']))"
"$L5PY" infra/tools/check_contract_inputs.py
```

SHAPE OK 5
L5-T04 SELF-VERIFY PASS: 5 contract inputs present
```

Spec anchors: §11 Organisation and Access · §40 Secrets and Production Access · §64 Safe Defaults and Fail-Closed Classification · §90 Access Control Architecture.

### L5-T05 — GitHub permission semantics as data (§11.1)
**Phase** P1 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T04 · **Spec** §11.1 (spec lines 817–838), §11.4 (D73)
**Files:** `access/model/github-permissions.yaml` (new)

```bash

START=$(grep -n "### 11.1 GitHub permission semantics" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+21))p" "$SPEC"      # source table - transcribe EVERY row
```

Author `access/model/github-permissions.yaml` with this exact top-level shape, one `capabilities` entry per printed row, cell values verbatim:

```yaml
source: "spec 11.1 GitHub permission semantics - verified, not assumed"
roles: [read, triage, write, maintain, admin]
capabilities:
  - capability: "View and clone code"
    read: yes
    triage: yes
    write: yes
    maintain: yes
    admin: yes
  # ... one entry per remaining row. Rows that carry free text rather than yes/no
  # (Merge into a protected branch; Deploy to a protected environment) keep the
  # cell text verbatim as a string instead of a boolean.
consequences:
  - id: cross-reviewer-needs-write
    statement: "A Cross-Reviewer holding only Read submits a review that does not satisfy branch protection; therefore Cross-Reviewers require Write."
  - id: least-privilege-boundary
    statement: "Least privilege is preserved by branch protection and the 27.2 workflow-identity gate, not by withholding Write."
plan_tier_facts:
  branch_protection_private_repos: team-plan-available
  environment_required_reviewers_private_repos: enterprise-only   # spec 11.4, D73 - never depended on
  custom_repository_roles: enterprise-cloud-only
  outside_collaborator_scoping: all-tiers
```

git add access/model/github-permissions.yaml

```

3. `plan_tier_facts.environment_required_reviewers_private_repos` is `enterprise-only` (§11.4, D73).
4. The file parses as YAML and contains no `TBD`, `TODO` or empty value.

```

Author `access/model/permission-model.yaml`:

```yaml
source: "spec 11.2 The permission model"
organisation:
  base_permission: read
  two_factor:
    enforced: true
    strong_factor_roles: [founder, organisation-owner, platform-admin]
    strong_factor: "hardware security key or passkey"
  second_owner_required: true      # spec 11.2 and invariant 39
write_grant_mechanism: teams-derived-from-registries
person_classes:                    # one entry per row of the 11.2 table (6 rows)
  - class: founder
    org_role: owner
    write_on: "All (implicit)"
    read_on: "All"
  - class: team-lead               # covers Acting Team Lead
    org_role: member
    write_on: "All product repositories"
    read_on: "All"
  # ... qa, developer, contractor-or-temporary-specialist, background-machine-layer
  # The background-machine-layer row must read verbatim:
  #   write_on: "Branch push only on whitelisted repositories; no merge, no environment access"
  #   read_on:  "Repositories in its queue"
teams:
  per_product: "One Team per product, named for the product, containing everyone holding a current assignment on it"
  org_wide: [team-lead-role, qa-role]
  derivation: registries
  reconciled: continuously         # a mismatch fails CI and raises a drift finding (spec 53)
```

4. `write_grant_mechanism` is `teams-derived-from-registries` — Write is never granted by role name (invariant 11).

### L5-T07 — Branch-protection declaration (§11.3)
**Phase** P1 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T05 · **Spec** §11.3 (spec lines 860–874), §33.4, §98.2 Phase 1
**Files:** `access/model/branch-protection.yaml` (new)

This file is the **declared** protection state. L3's reconciler compares actual GitHub state against it through `contracts/drift-finding.schema.json`. Lane 5 declares; Lane 3 reconciles. Write no reconciliation code here.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p1-t07-branch-protection
START=$(grep -n "### 11.3 Branch protection configuration" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+14))p" "$SPEC"
cat > access/model/branch-protection.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 11.3 Branch protection configuration, per repository"
default_branch_rules:
  require_pull_request: true
  required_approving_reviews: 1
  require_code_owner_review: true
  codeowners_contains_human_identities_only: true   # a machine approval can never satisfy the gate
  require_approval_of_most_recent_push: true        # the enforcement behind invariant 9, no self-approval
  dismiss_stale_approvals: true
  require_branches_up_to_date: true
  block_force_push: true
  block_deletion: true
  apply_to_administrators: true
  administrator_exception: "documented break-glass only, with an audit record (spec 54)"
required_status_checks:
  policy: "starts EMPTY per repository and is populated as each check comes into existence (spec 98.2 Phase 1)"
  contexts:
    - { context: tests, added_at_phase: 4 }
    - { context: build, added_at_phase: 6 }
    - { context: security-scan, added_at_phase: 6 }
    - { context: contract-validation, added_at_phase: 3 }
    - { context: reviewer-matrix-validation, added_at_phase: 3 }
    - { context: parity-check, added_at_phase: 4 }
    - { context: verification-contract, added_at_phase: 5 }
    - { context: control-plane/blocking-drift, added_at_phase: 3 }
codeowners_generation:
  generated_from: registries
  human_identities_only: true
  verification_paths: "owned by the holder of verification_responsibility"
  team_lead_owned: ["product.yaml", "migration directories", "CI workflow files"]
  product_source_paths: "owned by the product Team"
environments:
  deployment_branch_and_tag_policy_required: true   # spec 33.4 - governs what may reach environment secrets
  applies_to: [staging, production]
new_repository_defaults:
  visibility: private
  branch_protection_from_template_at_creation: true
  environment_access: none
  third_party_app_access: none
YAML
git add access/model/branch-protection.yaml
git commit -m "L5-T07: declare branch protection configuration (spec 11.3)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p1-t07-branch-protection
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. All eleven `default_branch_rules` booleans are present and `true` (`administrator_exception` is a string, not a boolean).
2. `required_status_checks.contexts` lists exactly the **8** contexts named in §11.3, each with its `added_at_phase`.
3. `codeowners_generation.human_identities_only` is `true`.
4. `environments.deployment_branch_and_tag_policy_required` is `true` — branch protection governs what merges, the deployment branch policy governs what reaches an environment's secrets; neither substitutes for the other.

**SELF-VERIFY**

"$L5PY" - <<'PY'

bools=[k for k,v in r.items() if isinstance(v,bool)]
assert len(bools)==11 and all(r[k] for k in bools), bools
assert len(d['required_status_checks']['contexts'])==8

assert d['new_repository_defaults']['visibility']=='private'
print('L5-T07 SELF-VERIFY PASS')

```

### L5-T08 — Five secret tiers and the fifth-tier envelope (§40.1)
**Phase** P1 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T05 · **Spec** §40.1 (spec lines 3644–3686), D76, D89, D107
**Files:** `access/model/secret-tiers.yaml` (new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p1-t08-secret-tiers
START=$(grep -n "### 40.1 Five secret tiers" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+42))p" "$SPEC"
cat > access/model/secret-tiers.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 40.1 Five secret tiers"
tiers:   # exactly five, in this order; a secret never moves down a tier
  - { id: developer,     location: ".env.local, git-ignored",                                   contains: "Local-only values. Never real production credentials" }
  - { id: ci,            location: "GitHub Actions secrets",                                    contains: "Build-time and scan credentials" }
  - { id: staging,       location: "GitHub Environment: staging",                               contains: "Staging-scoped credentials only" }
  - { id: production,    location: "GitHub Environment: production",                            contains: "Production credentials, accessible only to the production deployment workflow" }
  - { id: control-plane, location: "Machine-credential store on the hosts that use them",       contains: "Machine credentials, each a least-privilege GitHub App or fine-grained PAT" }
violation_rule: "A production credential appearing anywhere below the production tier is a security incident (spec 43), not a cleanup task"
repository_split:            # D89 - the credential cannot reach a registry
  control_plane:
    protection: "Full branch protection per 11.3"
    bypass_actor: none
    machine_write: "The reconciler's declared repair scope only (spec 26.4); a write outside it is Blocking drift"
  records:
    protection: "No review protection; a ruleset blocks force push, branch deletion and tag deletion, with no bypass actor (D107)"
    machine_write: "The records-writer credential: a GitHub App installation token whose fine-grained contents:write is scoped to this repository alone"
fifth_tier_credentials:      # each carries an asset-inventory entry (spec 49.1) - see L5-T34
  - id: reconciler
    extra_scope: "check-run write on product repositories, for exactly one named check (spec 40.1)"
  - id: provisioning-cli
  - id: organisation-export-token
  - id: records-writer
  - id: layer-b-backup
    note: "append-only object-store credential"
envelope_fields_required:    # spec 40.1 The behavioural envelope - all five mandatory per credential
  - signed_run_record_naming_the_scheduled_trigger
  - run_count_ceiling_per_day
  - expected_source_host
  - published_per_run_api_call_counts
  - named_owner_of_the_alert_configuration
rotation:
  cadence_initial: quarterly
  rotator_capability: devops
  runbook: "runbooks/credential-rotation.md   # control-plane repo, L0-owned path - Lane 5 does not write it"
  done_condition: "A manual reconciliation run completes clean AFTER rotation; a credential that rotates but no longer reconciles has been broken, not rotated"
  reissue_source_on_vm_loss: "spec 14.4 escrow"
records_append_only_controls:   # D107 - all four required
  - ruleset_blocks_rewrite_with_no_bypass_actor
  - commits_signed_by_the_records_writer_identity
  - head_sha_and_commit_count_anchored_into_the_control_plane_each_reconciliation_run
  - object_locked_organisation_export_as_the_slow_anchor
human_infrastructure_access: "provider-SSO-federated with no long-lived key (spec 40.2) - this is why there are exactly five tiers and not six"
YAML
git add access/model/secret-tiers.yaml
git commit -m "L5-T08: five secret tiers, fifth-tier envelope, records split (spec 40.1, D89, D107)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p1-t08-secret-tiers
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. `tiers` has exactly **5** entries with ids `developer, ci, staging, production, control-plane`, in that order.
2. `fifth_tier_credentials` names exactly the **5** credentials §40.1 lists.
3. `envelope_fields_required` has all **5** fields — a missing one makes the out-of-window alert unfirable, which is a gate that reads armed and is not.
4. `repository_split.control_plane.bypass_actor` is `none` (D89), and `records_append_only_controls` lists all **4** D107 controls.

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" - <<'PY'
import yaml
d=yaml.safe_load(open('access/model/secret-tiers.yaml'))
assert [t['id'] for t in d['tiers']]==['developer','ci','staging','production','control-plane']
assert sorted(c['id'] for c in d['fifth_tier_credentials'])==['layer-b-backup','organisation-export-token','provisioning-cli','reconciler','records-writer']
assert len(d['envelope_fields_required'])==5
assert d['repository_split']['control_plane']['bypass_actor']=='none'
assert len(d['records_append_only_controls'])==4
print('L5-T08 SELF-VERIFY PASS')
PY
```
Expected output (exact):
```
L5-T08 SELF-VERIFY PASS
```
**STOP** if any task, person or tool asks you to add a sixth tier. Human infrastructure access is provider-SSO-federated with no long-lived key (§40.2), which is precisely why the count is five. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T08`.

principals: [founder, team_lead, employee, peer]
layers:

mon=[x for x in c if x['control'].startswith('Product runtime monitoring')][0]
assert "dead-man" in str(mon).lower()
print('L5-T10 SELF-VERIFY PASS')
PY

Author the three files to this contract, exactly — no additional behaviour:

Each value is a JSON Schema requiring exactly the keys that task's acceptance criteria name, with `"additionalProperties": false` at its top level and `"required"` listing every top-level key of that model file.
- **`access/tools/validate_access_model.py`** — CLI. `--only <stem>` validates one file; no flag validates all seven. On success print `ACCESS MODEL OK: <n> file(s)` and exit `0`. On failure print `ACCESS MODEL FAIL: <path>: <jsonschema message>` and exit `1`. A model file that does not exist is a **failure**, never a skip (fail-closed, §64.2 row 1).
- **`access/tests/test_access_model.py`** — for each of the seven stems: one positive test asserting the shipped file validates, and one negative test that copies the file to a temp path, deletes one required top-level key, and asserts the validator exits `1`. **14 tests total.**

### L5-T14 — `people-intelligence` non-delegability checker (D109, AT-091)
**Phase** P1 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T12 · **Spec** §90.4, D109, invariant 106, AT-090, AT-091
**Files:** `access/tools/check_people_intelligence_gate.py`, `access/tests/test_pi_gate.py` (new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p1-t14-pi-gate
START=$(grep -n "### 90.4 The people-intelligence capability" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+9))p" "$SPEC"
```

The checker reads the people registry through the contract declared in `infra/contract-inputs.yaml` (`people-registry`) and enforces:

1. **Not delegable (D109).** Any assignment record whose effect grants `people-intelligence` fails validation. No assignment type grants it and none may be introduced — this is AT-090's clause "an attempt to create an assignment granting `people-intelligence` fails validation".
2. **Holder set.** The Founder holds it by default; the holder set is the only source of Layer B access (§90.4).
3. **Self-view carve-out.** A person opening their **own** Layer B-S self-view passes without holding the capability (AT-090, invariant 106).
4. **Peer block.** A non-holder requesting **another** person's Layer B data fails.
5. **Conduct carve-out.** Holding `people-intelligence` never grants conduct-record access (§90.4, §88).
6. Exit `1` printing `PI GATE FAIL: <reason>`; `--selftest` exercises all four cases and prints `L5-T14 SELF-VERIFY PASS`.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" access/tools/check_people_intelligence_gate.py --selftest
"$L5PY" -m pytest access/tests/test_pi_gate.py -q
git add access/tools/check_people_intelligence_gate.py access/tests/test_pi_gate.py
git commit -m "L5-T14: people-intelligence capability gate, non-delegable (D109, AT-091)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p1-t14-pi-gate
```

**Acceptance criteria**
1. A fixture assignment granting `people-intelligence` exits `1`.
2. Own-data self-view by a non-holder exits `0`.
3. Another person's data requested by a non-holder exits `1`.
4. Conduct-record access by a holder exits `1`.

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" access/tools/check_people_intelligence_gate.py --selftest
"$L5PY" -m pytest access/tests/test_pi_gate.py -q 2>&1 | tail -1
```
Expected output (exact, two lines):
```
L5-T14 SELF-VERIFY PASS
4 passed
```
**STOP** if `contracts/people.schema.json` is absent — L5-T04 should already have blocked. Never model the registry yourself. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T14`.

### L5-T16 — Ops-VM image and version lock  **[ASSISTED — L0 EXECUTES]**

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 & FD-112 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047; DevLake deferred from V1 per FD-112. Assisted task reserved for L0 Lead.

**Phase** P2 · **Size** S · **Mode** ASSISTED · **Deps** L5-T02 · **Spec** §99.5 (DevLake database engine and version confirmed before install), §51.4, invariant 85
**Files:** `ops-vm/compose/versions.lock`, `ops-vm/assisted/version-lock-request.md`, `ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py` (all new)

**Why ASSISTED:** a full image digest is read from a container registry the agent must not authenticate to, and §99.5 requires DevLake's database engine and version to be *confirmed for the pinned release before install* — a confirmation, not an inference. Inventing either value would pin the stack to a guess and silently determine the control-plane backup procedure.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t16-version-lock
mkdir -p ops-vm/assisted/evidence
cat > ops-vm/compose/versions.lock <<'YAML'
# Operations-VM component pin file. Invariant 85: every component is pinned by
# full image digest, never by tag. An empty digest is an UNFILLED SLOT, not a
# default: ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py exits 1 while any slot is empty.
# Filled by the L0 human lead (L5-T16 is ASSISTED). Never filled by an agent.
lock_version: 1
source: "spec 99.5 technology-shape decisions; spec 51.4 control-plane patching; invariant 85"
components:
  - { id: grafana,           image: "grafana/grafana",            digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: prometheus,        image: "prom/prometheus",            digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: devlake,           image: "apache/devlake",             digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: devlake-config-ui, image: "apache/devlake-config-ui",   digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: devlake-database,  image: "",                           digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: renovate,          image: "renovate/renovate",          digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
  - { id: scorecard,         image: "gcr.io/openssf/scorecard",   digest: "", pinned_by: digest }
devlake_database:
  # Spec 99.5: "Confirm DevLake's required database engine and version for the
  # pinned release before install - it determines the compose file and the
  # control-plane backup procedure." Declared, never inferred.
  engine: ""
  version: ""
  confirmed_against_devlake_release: ""
YAML
cat > ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py <<'PY'
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Fail closed while any pin slot in ops-vm/compose/versions.lock is unfilled.

--template-only  validate the STRUCTURE only (used before L0 fills the file).
no flag          require every digest, image, engine and version to be filled.
"""
import re, sys, pathlib, yaml
ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
LOCK = ROOT / "ops-vm" / "compose" / "versions.lock"
DIGEST = re.compile(r"^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
if not LOCK.exists():
    print("VERSION LOCK MISSING: ops-vm/compose/versions.lock"); sys.exit(1)
d = yaml.safe_load(LOCK.read_text())
comps = d.get("components") or []
db = d.get("devlake_database") or {}
structural = []
if len(comps) != 7:
    structural.append("components must hold exactly 7 entries, found %d" % len(comps))
for c in comps:
    for k in ("id", "image", "digest", "pinned_by"):
        if k not in c:
            structural.append("component %r missing key %s" % (c.get("id"), k))
    if c.get("pinned_by") != "digest":
        structural.append("component %r is not pinned_by: digest" % c.get("id"))
for k in ("engine", "version", "confirmed_against_devlake_release"):
    if k not in db:
        structural.append("devlake_database missing key %s" % k)
if structural:
    for s in structural:
        print("VERSION LOCK STRUCTURE FAIL: " + s)
    sys.exit(1)
if "--template-only" in sys.argv:
    print("L5-T16 TEMPLATE OK"); sys.exit(0)
unfilled = []
for c in comps:
    if not str(c.get("image", "")).strip():
        unfilled.append("components[%s].image" % c.get("id"))
    if not DIGEST.match(str(c.get("digest", "")).strip()):
        unfilled.append("components[%s].digest" % c.get("id"))
for k in ("engine", "version", "confirmed_against_devlake_release"):
    if not str(db.get(k, "")).strip():
        unfilled.append("devlake_database.%s" % k)
if unfilled:
    print("VERSION LOCK UNFILLED: %d field(s)" % len(unfilled))
    for u in unfilled:
        print("  " + u)
    sys.exit(1)
print("VERSION LOCK OK: 7 components pinned by digest")
PY
cat > ops-vm/assisted/version-lock-request.md <<'MD'
# ASSISTED REQUEST — L5-T16: fill the operations-VM version lock

Executed by the **L0 human lead**. An agent prepares this file and stops here.

| # | Value to supply | Where it goes | Spec | How it is obtained |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Grafana image digest | `components[grafana].digest` | invariant 85 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 2 | Prometheus image digest | `components[prometheus].digest` | invariant 85 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 3 | DevLake image digest | `components[devlake].digest` | invariant 85, §99.5 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 4 | DevLake config-UI image digest | `components[devlake-config-ui].digest` | invariant 85 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 5 | DevLake database image **and** digest | `components[devlake-database].image`, `.digest` | §99.5 | determined by row 8 below |
| 6 | Renovate image digest | `components[renovate].digest` | invariant 85 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 7 | Scorecard image digest | `components[scorecard].digest` | invariant 85 | registry digest for the chosen release |
| 8 | DevLake required database **engine** and **version** | `devlake_database.engine`, `.version` | §99.5 | confirmed against the pinned DevLake release before install |
| 9 | The DevLake release the confirmation was made against | `devlake_database.confirmed_against_devlake_release` | §99.5 | recorded so the confirmation is re-checkable at the next pin move |

**Rules that bind the filler as well as the agent**

1. A tag is not a pin. Only `sha256:<64 hex>` is accepted (invariant 85).
2. The engine/version pair determines the compose file **and** the control-plane backup procedure (§99.5). Changing it later is a platform change under §61, not an edit.
3. Evidence of the confirmation goes in `ops-vm/assisted/evidence/`, named `version-lock-evidence-<UTC-date>.md`.

Verify after filling: `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py` must print `VERSION LOCK OK: 7 components pinned by digest`.
MD
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py --template-only
git add ops-vm/compose/versions.lock ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py ops-vm/assisted
git commit -m "L5-T16: ASSISTED version-lock template, request document and fail-closed verifier"
git push -u origin lane/5/p2-t16-version-lock
gh issue create --title "ASSISTED L5-T16: fill the operations-VM version lock (registry digests + DevLake DB engine/version)" \
  --label "lane-5,assisted,l0" \
  --body "Agent-prepared, human-executed. The lead fills the nine slots listed in ops-vm/assisted/version-lock-request.md and records the confirmation evidence. Verify with: ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py"
```

**Acceptance criteria**

| # | Criterion | Proving command | Unambiguous output |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The template's structure is valid | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py --template-only` | `L5-T16 TEMPLATE OK` |
| 2 | The unfilled lock fails closed with an exact slot count | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py \| head -1` | `VERSION LOCK UNFILLED: 11 field(s)` |
| 3 | The unfilled lock exits non-zero | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"` | `rc=1` |
| 4 | No tag-shaped pin was committed | `grep -cE 'digest: *"(latest|v?[0-9])' ops-vm/compose/versions.lock` | `0` |
| 5 | The request document lists all nine supplied values | `grep -c '^| [0-9] |' ops-vm/assisted/version-lock-request.md` | `9` |

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py --template-only
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py | head -1
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/check_versions_lock.py >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"
grep -cE 'digest: *"(latest|v?[0-9])' ops-vm/compose/versions.lock
grep -c '^| [0-9] |' ops-vm/assisted/version-lock-request.md
```

VERSION LOCK UNFILLED: 11 field(s)
rc=1
0
9
```
The count of 11 is: seven empty `digest` values, one empty `image` value (`devlake-database`, whose image is determined by the engine confirmation of row 8), and the three `devlake_database` keys.

### L5-T17 — Compose stack rendered from the lock

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 & FD-112 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047; DevLake deferred from V1 per FD-112. Authoritative Ops-VM stack in L5-04 (30-stack-up.sh).

**Phase** P2 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T16 · **Spec** §51.4 (private path, every surface behind SSO), §51.5, §99.5, invariant 85, D75
**Files:** `ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml`, `ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py` (new)

The compose file is **generated**, never hand-edited: a hand edit is how a floating tag re-enters a pinned stack. `render_compose.py` is the only writer; `--check` is what CI and this lane's readiness report run.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t17-compose
```

Author `ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py` to this contract, exactly — no additional behaviour:

- It reads `ops-vm/compose/versions.lock` and nothing else. It never contacts a registry.
- **Fail-closed:** if `check_versions_lock.py`'s filled-mode rules do not hold, it prints `COMPOSE BLOCKED: versions.lock unfilled` and exits `1`. It never renders a partial stack.
- It renders exactly seven services, one per lock component, each with `image: "<image>@<digest>"` — no `:tag` form anywhere in the output.
- Every published port is written as `"127.0.0.1:<host>:<container>"`. A bare `"<host>:<container>"` publication is never emitted (§51.4: the SSO proxy is the sole ingress and it binds to the private address).
- It emits **no** people datasource, no Layer B volume and no Layer B network. Layer B is a separate compose project on a separate host (D75, L5-T46).
- It writes a first line `# GENERATED BY ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py FROM ops-vm/compose/versions.lock - DO NOT EDIT`.
- `--write` writes the file. `--check` re-renders in memory and byte-compares: identical prints `COMPOSE IN SYNC` and exits `0`; different prints `COMPOSE DRIFT: ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` and exits `1`.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py --write
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py --check
git add ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml
git commit -m "L5-T17: render the shared ops-VM compose stack from the digest lock"
git push -u origin lane/5/p2-t17-compose
```

**Acceptance criteria**

| # | Criterion | Proving command | Unambiguous output |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The committed file matches a fresh render | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py --check` | `COMPOSE IN SYNC` |
| 2 | Exactly seven images, every one digest-pinned | `grep -c '@sha256:' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` | `7` |
| 3 | No image is tag-pinned | `grep -cE 'image: "[^"@]+:[^"@]+"$' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` | `0` |
| 4 | No port is published on a wildcard address | `grep -cE '^ *- "[0-9]+:[0-9]+"' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` | `0` |
| 5 | The shared stack carries no people surface (D75, AT-097) | `grep -ci 'people' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` | `0` |
| 6 | The file declares itself generated | `head -1 ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml \| grep -c 'DO NOT EDIT'` | `1` |

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/render_compose.py --check
grep -c '@sha256:' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml
grep -cE 'image: "[^"@]+:[^"@]+"$' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml
grep -cE '^ *- "[0-9]+:[0-9]+"' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml
grep -ci 'people' ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml
head -1 ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml | grep -c 'DO NOT EDIT'
```
Expected output (exact, six lines):
```
COMPOSE IN SYNC
7
0
0
0
1
```
**STOP** if `COMPOSE BLOCKED: versions.lock unfilled` prints. That means L5-T16's ASSISTED half has not been executed. Do not fill a digest, do not comment out a service, do not render six of seven. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T17` naming `L5-T16` as the unmet dependency.

### L5-T18 — Host provisioning and fifth-tier credential isolation

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 & FD-112 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047; DevLake deferred per FD-112. Authoritative host provisioning in L5-04 (ops-vm/provision/30-stack-up.sh).

**Phase** P2 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T08, L5-T17 · **Spec** §51.4 (remote access, private path, SSO), §51.5 (what the VM holds and does not hold), §40.1 fifth tier, D95
**Files:** `ops-vm/provision/provision.sh`, `ops-vm/provision/hardening.yaml`, `ops-vm/tests/test_hardening.py` (new)

`provision.sh` is written here and executed **by a human** in L5-T23. It must be idempotent — §51.5 makes the VM disposable, and a rebuild is a replay, never a fresh design.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t18-provision
cat > ops-vm/provision/hardening.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 51.4 control-plane patching and remote access; spec 51.5 the disposable operations VM; spec 40.1 fifth tier; D95"
assertions:
  - id: no-public-bind
    statement: "No control-plane surface listens on a wildcard address. Every surface binds to the private address only."
    spec: "51.4"
  - id: sso-in-front
    statement: "Every control-plane surface sits behind the SSO front door on the private network path (VPN or mesh)."
    spec: "51.4"
  - id: exposure-is-a-decision
    statement: "Network exposure of any control-plane surface is a named security decision with the Founder as decider, recorded like any other decision - never a convenience default."
    spec: "51.4"
  - id: no-production-credentials
    statement: "The VM holds no production credentials, no deploy keys and no environment access."
    spec: "51.5"
  - id: holds-fifth-tier-store
    statement: "The VM does hold the fifth-tier machine-credential store, including the reconciler credential and the organisation-export token. This is why the second trust boundary of 40.3 exists."
    spec: "51.5, 40.1"
  - id: fifth-tier-not-passively-readable
    statement: "The fifth-tier store is held so that VM-root does not passively read it: encrypted at rest with a key that is not resident on the host's disk, and readable only by the dedicated service user that uses it."
    spec: "D95, 40.3"
  - id: no-background-layer-here
    statement: "The background machine layer does not run on the operations VM. It runs on its own dedicated host and the two hosts share no credentials."
    spec: "51.5"
  - id: no-ci-runner-here
    statement: "No self-hosted CI runner is located on the operations VM; 45.2's claim that CI keeps running when the VM is lost depends on that placement."
    spec: "49.1, 45.2"
  - id: patched-on-declared-cadence
    statement: "The host OS and every stack component are patched on the cadence declared in ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml."
    spec: "51.4"
private_network_path:
  mechanism: "DECLARED-BY-L0"   # VPN or mesh; a named security decision with the Founder as decider (51.4)
  decision_record: "DECLARED-BY-L0"
  note: "provision.sh refuses to run while either value is DECLARED-BY-L0. An implementer never fills this."
YAML
cat > ops-vm/provision/provision.sh <<'SH'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Operations-VM base provisioning. Idempotent: a rebuild is a replay (spec 51.5).
# Executed by the L0 human lead on the host (L5-T23). Never by an agent.
set -euo pipefail
HARDENING="ops-vm/provision/hardening.yaml"
[ -f "$HARDENING" ] || { echo "PROVISION REFUSED: missing $HARDENING"; exit 1; }
if grep -q 'DECLARED-BY-L0' "$HARDENING"; then
  echo "PROVISION REFUSED: the private network path is an undeclared security decision (spec 51.4)."
  echo "DECISION REQUIRED FROM L0: name the VPN-or-mesh mechanism and its decision record id."
  exit 1
fi
echo "STEP 01 host baseline packages"
echo "STEP 02 dedicated service users, one per stack component"
echo "STEP 03 private network path enrolment"
echo "STEP 04 SSO front door, bound to the private address only"
echo "STEP 05 fifth-tier credential store, mode 0600, dedicated owner, encrypted at rest"
echo "STEP 06 firewall: deny inbound except the private path"
echo "STEP 07 compose stack from ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml"
echo "PROVISION PLAN OK: 7 step(s)"
SH
chmod +x ops-vm/provision/provision.sh
```

Author `ops-vm/tests/test_hardening.py` with exactly **9 tests**, one per `assertions[].id`, each asserting that the assertion is present, carries a non-empty `statement` and cites a spec section. Add no tenth test and no assertion the spec does not carry.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
bash -n ops-vm/provision/provision.sh && echo SYNTAX-OK
"$L5PY" -m pytest ops-vm/tests/test_hardening.py -q
git add ops-vm/provision ops-vm/tests/test_hardening.py
git commit -m "L5-T18: ops-VM hardening declaration, provisioning plan and static tests"
git push -u origin lane/5/p2-t18-provision
```

> **DECISION REQUIRED (route to L0, do not decide):** §51.4 makes the private-path mechanism (VPN or mesh) and any network exposure **a named security decision with the Founder as decider**. Lane 5 declares the slot and fails closed on it; Lane 5 never picks the mechanism. Raise it on the L5-T23 handoff issue with the line: *"Name the private network path mechanism and its decision record id; `ops-vm/provision/provision.sh` refuses to run until both are recorded."*

**Acceptance criteria**

| # | Criterion | Proving command | Unambiguous output |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The script is syntactically valid | `bash -n ops-vm/provision/provision.sh && echo SYNTAX-OK` | `SYNTAX-OK` |

| 3 | The script refuses to run on an undeclared private path | `bash ops-vm/provision/provision.sh >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"` | `rc=1` |
| 4 | No credential value was committed | `grep -cEi '(password|token|secret) *[:=] *[^ "]' ops-vm/provision/provision.sh` | `0` |
| 5 | The script never opens a remote session | `grep -cE '^[^#]*\b(ssh|scp|rsync)\b' ops-vm/provision/provision.sh` | `0` |

bash ops-vm/provision/provision.sh >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"

Author `ops-vm/slo/platform-slos.yaml` with this exact top-level shape, one `objectives` entry per printed row, cell values verbatim:

```yaml
source: "spec 51.2 Platform SLOs - indicative starting values, to be calibrated"
objectives:
  - id: ci-availability
    service: "CI availability"
    objective: "99% of pushes produce a completed run within SLA"
    measurement: "Workflow run success and duration"
    response_on_breach: "Team Lead; consider runner capacity. Blocking where it prevents an incident fix; loss of the execution estate enters degraded engineering mode (Section 46.6)"
  - id: control-plane-recovery-time

    objective: "Control-plane repository and registries restorable within 4 hours"
    measurement: "Quarterly restore test"
    response_on_breach: "Treated as a product-grade restore obligation"

    service: "Operations-VM liveness, observed off-VM"

  # ... one entry per remaining row: deployment-pipeline-availability,
  # reconciliation-freshness, health-report-freshness, drift-detection-freshness,
  # background-queue-availability, devlake-ingest-freshness. Nine in total.
error_budget_rule: "When a control-plane SLO is exhausted, the platform change budget for that month is reduced (Section 59). The control plane is stabilised before it is extended."
console_rule: "Where the founder ops console answers a question about SLO state, its answers are read-only summaries sourced from the same recorded signals the alerting reads. The console is never itself an alert source and never a monitoring dependency; every objective above is measured and escalated by the self-hosted monitoring stack whether or not the console exists."
```

Author `ops-vm/tools/validate_slos.py`: it loads the file and asserts (1) exactly 9 objectives, (2) each carries a non-empty `service`, `objective`, `measurement` and `response_on_breach`, (3) `control-plane-recovery-time`'s objective names 4 hours, (4) `operations-vm-liveness-off-vm`'s response names both the messaging channel and the phone path and contains the phrase `never through the VM`, (5) `error_budget_rule` and `console_rule` are present. ### L5-T20 — Patch cadence and post-patch smoke checklist (§51.4)

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047. Authoritative smoke checks in ops-vm/checks/post-patch-smoke.sh and access/layer-b/check-post-patch.sh per L5-04/05.

**Phase** P2 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T17, L5-T19 · **Spec** §51.4 (spec lines 4487–4499), §90.4, §61 (singleton change shape)
**Files:** `ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml`, `ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py` (new)

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t20-patching
START=$(grep -n "### 51.4 Control-plane patching" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+12))p" "$SPEC"
cat > ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 51.4 Control-plane patching"
rule: "Renovate patches what lives in repositories; the control-plane stack itself needs its own declared patch cadence, tracked through the tool register (spec 62)."
staleness:
  beyond_cadence: "Amber condition on operating-system health"
  known_exploited_vulnerability_in_a_deployed_version: "Red"
expedited_path:
  applies_to: [grafana, vm-operating-system]
  rule: "Security patches are applied on an expedited path, not batched to the routine window."
components:   # one row per component of ops-vm/compose/versions.lock, plus the host OS
  - { id: grafana,           cadence: monthly,   tier: elevated, reason: "Layer B makes part of the Grafana estate a store of sensitive people data (spec 51.4)" }
  - { id: grafana-layer-b,   cadence: tightest,  tier: tightest, reason: "The separately-credentialed Layer B datasource is patched and reviewed on the tightest cadence in the stack (spec 51.4)" }
  - { id: prometheus,        cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: devlake,           cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: devlake-config-ui, cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: devlake-database,  cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: renovate,          cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: scorecard,         cadence: monthly,   tier: routine,  reason: "control-plane stack component" }
  - { id: vm-operating-system, cadence: monthly, tier: elevated, reason: "expedited security path per spec 51.4" }
change_shape:   # spec 51.4 The singleton change shape
  stages: [snapshot, upgrade, verify, revert-on-fail]
  canary: none
  reason: "The control plane has no canary set - there is one Grafana, one DevLake, one operations VM."
  manifest_and_approval: "carries the Section 61 change manifest and approval, but no canary stage"
  rollback_answer: "the snapshot, known before the upgrade starts"
completion_rule: "The patch is not recorded as complete until the post-patch smoke checklist and the mandated immediate reconciliation run both pass clean."
YAML
```

Author `ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py`. It has one behaviour and no other: it prints the checklist as **5 ordered steps** and, under `--dry-run`, performs none of them.

1. `dashboards provision from JSON`
2. `both instances' datasources connect`
3. `alert rules fire a test alert`
4. `the Founder-only Layer B instance's credential and authentication allowlist match the capability holders (spec 90.4)`
5. `an immediate reconciliation run completes clean (mandated by spec 51.4)`

`--dry-run` prints one `SMOKE STEP n/5: <text>` line per step, then `POST-PATCH SMOKE DRY-RUN OK: 5 step(s)`, and exits `0`. Without `--dry-run` it exits `1` printing `POST-PATCH SMOKE REFUSED: live execution is host-side and belongs to L5-T23 (ASSISTED)` — an agent never runs the live checklist.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py --dry-run
git add ops-vm/patching ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py
git commit -m "L5-T20: control-plane patch cadence and the post-patch smoke checklist (spec 51.4)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p2-t20-patching
```

**Acceptance criteria**

| # | Criterion | Proving command | Unambiguous output |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The checklist has exactly five ordered steps | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py --dry-run \| tail -1` | `POST-PATCH SMOKE DRY-RUN OK: 5 step(s)` |
| 2 | Live execution is refused to an agent | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"` | `rc=1` |
| 3 | Every stack component plus the host OS carries a cadence | `"$L5PY" -c "import yaml;print(len(yaml.safe_load(open('ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml'))['components']))"` | `9` |
| 4 | The Layer B datasource carries the tightest cadence | `grep -c 'tier: tightest' ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml` | `1` |
| 5 | The singleton change shape carries no canary stage | `grep -c 'canary: none' ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml` | `1` |

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash

"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py --dry-run | tail -1
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/post_patch_smoke.py >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"
"$L5PY" -c "import yaml;print(len(yaml.safe_load(open('ops-vm/patching/patch-cadence.yaml'))['components']))"

### L5-T21 — Off-VM detection leg (§51.5, D94)

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047. Authoritative implementation in ops-vm/deadman/ per L5-04.

**Phase** P2 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T19 · **Spec** §51.5 (spec lines 4500–4509), §42.2, §92.11, D94, invariant 52
**Files:** `ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml`, `ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml`, `ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py` (new)

D94 states the rule this task exists for: *"Detection does not depend on the machine it is supposed to be independent of."* The heartbeat leaves the VM; the witness is outside the VM, outside GitHub and outside the product providers; the alarm reaches the §42.2 phone path directly.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t21-offvm
START=$(grep -n "### 51.5 The disposable operations VM" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+9))p" "$SPEC"
cat > ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 51.5 The disposable operations VM; D94; spec 42.2 phone-escalation path"
rule: "Prometheus, alert evaluation and Grafana all die with the host. A stack that watches only itself reports nothing in exactly the way a healthy estate does."
heartbeat:
  emitted_by: "the monitoring stack on the operations VM"
  emitted_to: witness
  interval_seconds: 300
  miss_threshold: 2
witness:
  id: "DECLARED-BY-L0"
  outside_the_operations_vm: true
  outside_github: true            # D94 - the witness may not be a GitHub Actions schedule
  outside_the_product_providers: true
  named_owner: "capability:devops"
  asset_inventory_entry: offvm-deadman-heartbeat     # assets/inventory/offvm-deadman-heartbeat.yaml (L5-T34)
on_miss:
  class: Red
  routes: ["messaging-channel", "phone-path"]
  never_through_the_vm: true
  spec: "51.2 Operations-VM liveness row; 42.2"
drill:
  cadence: quarterly
  method: "verified by stopping the VM, not merely by rebuilding it (spec 51.5)"
YAML
cat > ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml <<'YAML'
source: "spec 51.5 - an external uptime check per product hitting /health from outside the VM"
generation:
  targets_from: registries        # invariant 52 - product count is never hard-coded
  hand_listed_targets_permitted: false
check:
  path: "/health"
  executed_from: "outside the operations VM"
  interval_seconds: 60
  failure_threshold: 3
on_failure:
  class: Red
  routes: ["messaging-channel", "phone-path"]
  never_through_the_vm: true
named_owner: "capability:devops"
asset_inventory_entry: offvm-uptime-check           # assets/inventory/offvm-uptime-check.yaml (L5-T34)
YAML
```

Author `ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py`:

- `--dry-run` renders the heartbeat payload it *would* send, prints the resolved witness id and the on-miss route, makes **no network call**, and exits `0` with the final line `DEADMAN DRY-RUN OK: witness=<id> route=messaging-channel+phone-path`.
- **Fail-closed:** if `witness.id` is `DECLARED-BY-L0`, or `outside_github` is not `true`, it prints `DEADMAN BLOCKED: <reason>` and exits `1`.
- It contains no hard-coded product list and no hard-coded destination (invariant 52; §92.11 "a configuration value, never a hard-coded destination").
- It never sends for real. Live emission runs on the host under L5-T23.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py --dry-run
git add ops-vm/offvm ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py
git commit -m "L5-T21: off-VM detection leg - dead-man heartbeat and per-product uptime checks (D94)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p2-t21-offvm
```

> **DECISION REQUIRED (route to L0, do not decide):** D94 requires a witness **outside the VM, outside GitHub and outside the product providers**. Choosing that witness is a provider selection with a cost and a credential; it is not yours. `witness.id` therefore ships as `DECLARED-BY-L0` and the tool fails closed on it. Raise it on the L5-T23 handoff issue with the line: *"Name the off-VM dead-man witness — it may not be a GitHub Actions schedule (D94) — and record it as `witness.id` in `ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml`."* This is also the reason **L5-T58** requests no workflow for this job.

**Acceptance criteria**

| # | Criterion | Proving command | Unambiguous output |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The tool fails closed on the undeclared witness | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py --dry-run >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"` | `rc=1` |
| 2 | It names the reason rather than defaulting | `"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py --dry-run 2>&1 \| head -1` | `DEADMAN BLOCKED: witness.id is DECLARED-BY-L0` |
| 3 | The witness is required to sit outside GitHub (D94) | `grep -c 'outside_github: true' ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml` | `1` |
| 4 | Neither leg routes through the VM | `grep -c 'never_through_the_vm: true' ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml \| grep -c ':1'` | `2` |
| 5 | No product is hard-coded (invariant 52) | `grep -c 'targets_from: registries' ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml` | `1` |
| 6 | The tool makes no network call | `grep -cE '^[^#]*(requests\.|urllib|socket\.)' ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py` | `0` |

**SELF-VERIFY**
```bash
set -euo pipefail
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py --dry-run >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "rc=$?"
"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py --dry-run 2>&1 | head -1
grep -c 'outside_github: true' ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml
grep -c 'never_through_the_vm: true' ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml | grep -c ':1'
grep -c 'targets_from: registries' ops-vm/offvm/uptime-checks.yaml
grep -cE '^[^#]*(requests\.|urllib|socket\.)' ops-vm/tools/deadman_heartbeat.py
```
Expected output (exact, six lines):
```
rc=1
DEADMAN BLOCKED: witness.id is DECLARED-BY-L0
1
2
1
0
```
**STOP** if anyone proposes running the dead-man's-switch as a GitHub Actions schedule, or on the operations VM. Both defeat the leg D94 exists to create. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T21` quoting D94.

### L5-T22 — Rebuild runbook source and the 4-hour clock (§45.4)
**Phase** P2 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T17, L5-T18 · **Spec** §45.4 (spec lines 4078–4085), §45.2, §51.2 recovery objective, §51.5
**Files:** `ops-vm/rebuild/runbook.src.md`, `ops-vm/tools/rebuild_clock.py`, `ops-vm/rebuild/L0-HANDOFF.md` (new)

`docs/control-plane-rebuild.md` is the first-class artifact §45.4 names — and `docs/**` is **L0's path** (PARTITION line 22). Lane 5 therefore writes the *source*, proves it executable-as-written, and hands it over. Do not create `docs/control-plane-rebuild.md`; the lane guard will fail the PR.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p2-t22-rebuild
START=$(grep -n "### 45.4 Control-plane rebuild" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+7))p" "$SPEC"
```

Author `ops-vm/rebuild/runbook.src.md`. It is a numbered step list. **Every step names a command or a named human action; a step naming neither is a test failure, not an operator improvisation (§45.4).** It must contain, verbatim as headings:

- `## CLOCK START — provision-start`
- `## CLOCK STOP — dashboards-green, including the Layer B restore`
- `## ASSUMPTION — GitHub is reachable, because the reconstruction set lives there (45.1)`
- `## ALTERNATE PATH — organisation is unavailable: rebuild from the organisation export against the separate target declared in 45.2`
- `## LAYER B RESTORE — performed personally by the Founder, or a named person the Founder designates in writing`

The step list is fixed at **14 steps**, in this order — do not add, merge or reorder them:

| Step | Action | Source |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Provision a fresh host | §51.5 |
| 2 | Enrol the host on the private network path | §51.4 |
| 3 | Run `ops-vm/provision/provision.sh` | L5-T18 |
| 4 | Stand up the SSO front door | §51.4 |
| 5 | Restore the fifth-tier credential store from escrow | §40.1, §14.4 |
| 6 | Fetch the reconstruction set from GitHub | §45.1 |
| 7 | Bring up the shared stack from `ops-vm/compose/docker-compose.yml` | L5-T17 |
| 8 | Provision Grafana datasources and dashboards from JSON | §51.4 |
| 9 | Restore the metrics volume | §51.5 |
| 10 | Restore the Layer B people-data store | §51.4, §45.4 |
| 11 | Verify the Layer B store by decrypt-plus-checksum-manifest, without opening any document | §45.4 |
| 12 | Re-arm the off-VM detection leg and confirm the first heartbeat | §51.5, D94 |
| 13 | Run one full reconciliation to clean | §51.4 |
| 14 | Confirm dashboards-green and stop the clock | §45.4, §51.2 |

Author `ops-vm/tools/rebuild_clock.py` with `--validate <path>`. It asserts: the five headings above are present verbatim; the budget is declared as `240` minutes; every numbered step line is followed within its block by a fenced command or a line beginning `HUMAN:`; the Layer B restore block states the integrity check is `decrypt-plus-checksum-manifest` and that it is performed `without opening any document`; and that no machine identity is named as an actor in the Layer B block. On success it prints `REBUILD CLOCK OK: <n> steps, budget 240 min`; on failure `REBUILD CLOCK FAIL: <reason>` and exit `1`.

Author `ops-vm/rebuild/L0-HANDOFF.md`: one page naming the source path, the target path `docs/control-plane-rebuild.md`, the owning lane (L0), the validator command, and the statement that Lane 5 will not create the target.

"$L5PY" ops-vm/tools/rebuild_clock.py --validate ops-vm/rebuild/runbook.src.md
bash infra/tools/lane_guard_check.sh integration
git add ops-vm/rebuild ops-vm/tools/rebuild_clock.py

  --label "lane-5,l0,handoff" \

Validate with: ops-vm/tools/rebuild_clock.py --validate ops-vm/rebuild/runbook.src.md"
```

Author `ops-vm/assisted/vm-buildout-request.md` containing exactly this table, with an empty evidence column for the human to fill:

| # | Action | Required end state | Spec | Evidence to record |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Provision the operations VM | host exists; `ops-vm/provision/provision.sh` has run clean and is re-runnable | §51.5 | provider resource id + run log filename |

| 7 | Create the object-locked, versioned backup bucket | in a **different provider and credential domain** than the operations VM; object lock and versioning both on | §51.4, §45.3 | bucket ARN/id + lock configuration export |
| 8 | Create the append-only, write-only backup credential | it can write; it can neither read nor destroy backup history | §51.4 | negative-test transcript for read and delete |
| 9 | Name the off-VM dead-man witness | not the operations VM, not GitHub, not a product provider; written into `ops-vm/offvm/deadman.yaml` | §51.5, D94 | witness id + first heartbeat received |
| 10 | Run the post-patch smoke checklist once, live | all five steps pass clean | §51.4 | checklist output |
| 11 | Execute the rebuild drill against the runbook source | completes inside 240 minutes, Layer B restore included | §45.4, §51.2 | start/stop timestamps + drill record id |
| 12 | Destroy the drill copy on evidence | provider post-drill resource listing for the drill tag shows **zero resources**, and volume snapshots and provider-side backups are explicitly deleted | §45.4 | zero-resources listing |

## 8. TASK DETAIL — PHASE P3, SUBSYSTEM K (AI RUNTIME CONTRACT ENFORCEMENT)

Spec anchors: §35.1 Required runtime capabilities · §35.2 Approved runtime list · §35.5 Model regression benchmark · §35.6 Vendor data-handling checklist · §36.1 The constitutional rule · §36.3 Approved extension and MCP-server list · §36.4 Per-product AI restrictions · §36.6 Pre-flight secret stripping and the no-API-keys check · §38.3 Evaluation suites · invariants 84 and 85 · AT-107.

**PARTITION GAP note, binding on P3:** subsystem **J** (Background Machine Layer, §37) is unassigned in PARTITION v1. §35.2, §35.5, §36.3 and §36.6 all describe Hermes Agent hosts. In this phase Lane 5 writes **only** the configuration-enforcement half that lives in `access/**`: the approved-runtime and approved-extension lists, the pin checker, the no-API-keys check, the constitution reference check, the secret-strip pre-flight and the eval-runner CLI. Lane 5 writes **no** cage configuration file, **no** background-host egress override, **no** systemd stop unit and **no** local inference endpoint service. If a task appears to require one, that is the gap — state it and route to L0, never claim it.

### L5-T24 — Runtime capability and vendor checklist config (§35.1, §35.6)

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047. Authoritative AI toolchain in access/ai-toolchain/ per L5-05 (validate_toolchain.py).

**Phase** P3 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T12 · **Spec** §35.1 (spec lines 3057–3070), §35.2, §35.6 (spec lines 3135–3154), §36.3 (spec lines 3178–3208), D69
**Files:** `access/ai-runtime/runtime-contract.yaml`, `access/ai-runtime/vendor-checklist.yaml`, `access/ai-runtime/tools/validate_runtime_contract.py` (new)

`runtime-contract.yaml` is the single configuration file the rest of P3 reads. It carries three blocks: the §35.1 capability table, the §35.2 approved-runtime list, and the §36.3 per-runtime toolchain pins. It carries **no credential value of any kind**.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p3-t24-runtime-contract
mkdir -p access/ai-runtime/tools access/ai-runtime/benchmark
START=$(grep -n "### 35.1 Required runtime capabilities" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+13))p" "$SPEC"     # 35.1 capability table - 7 rows, 5 required + 2 optional
START=$(grep -n "### 35.6 Vendor data-handling" "$SPEC" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1)
sed -n "${START},$((START+19))p" "$SPEC"     # 35.6 vendor checklist - 7 checks
```

Author `access/ai-runtime/runtime-contract.yaml` with this exact top-level shape, cell values verbatim:

```yaml
source: "spec 35.1 required runtime capabilities; spec 35.2 approved runtime list; spec 36.3 approved extension and MCP-server list"
capabilities:            # one entry per row of the 35.1 table - 7 rows
  - { capability: "Planning assistance",            needed_for: "Discuss and Plan phases",           required: true }
  - { capability: "Code generation",                needed_for: "Execute phase",                     required: true }
  - { capability: "Debugging assistance",           needed_for: "Daily work",                        required: true }
  - { capability: "Repository file access",         needed_for: "All phases",                        required: true }
  - { capability: "Terminal or command execution",  needed_for: "Execute and verification phases",   required: true }
  - { capability: "Review assistance",              needed_for: "Optional, useful",                  required: false }
  - { capability: "Remote or mobile session control", needed_for: "Optional convenience",            required: false }
optional_rows_rule: "The optional rows are conveniences that never become dependencies."
approved_runtimes:       # spec 35.2 - exactly six
  - { id: claude-code,   caveat: none }
  - { id: codex,         caveat: none }
  - { id: antigravity,   caveat: none }
  - { id: cursor,        caveat: none }
  - { id: kilo-code,     caveat: "must be configured against a subscription provider, not its own credit-based billing, which is metered by default (spec 35.2)" }
  - { id: hermes-agent,  caveat: "approved under its local-inference-only profile; the harness, cage and inference service are subsystem J and are not built by lane 5" }
seat_rules:
  one_runtime_per_person: true
  minimum_hold: quarter
  mid_quarter_switch: "an exception with a reason, not a preference"
  fixed_cost_constraint: "Every engineering-tooling component is free, self-hosted, or a flat per-person subscription. Metered spend is prohibited for tooling."
  subscription_authentication_may_be_proxied: false
toolchain:               # spec 36.3 shape - pinned lists, empty by default
  claude-code:   { approved_extensions: [], approved_mcp_servers: [] }
  codex:         { approved_extensions: [], approved_mcp_servers: [] }
  antigravity:   { approved_extensions: [], approved_mcp_servers: [] }
  cursor:        { approved_extensions: [], approved_mcp_servers: [] }
  kilo-code:     { approved_extensions: [], approved_mcp_servers: [] }
  hermes-agent:
    source_commit: "8e9459c97f707047be5915a5c8b4c503756daa9b"   # D69 - full SHA, never a tag
    python_environment: "pinned by the repository lockfile at that commit"
    install_method: pinned-checkout-from-org-mirror
    self_update_permitted: false
    approved_extensions: []
    approved_mcp_servers: []
pin_rule: "Every extension and MCP server is pinned by full commit SHA or content checksum, never by tag - a tag is movable and is therefore not a pin (spec 36.3, invariant 85)."
model_artefact_pin:
  weights_pinned_by: content-checksum
  tokenizer_pinned_by: content-checksum
  mirrored_into_company_organisation: true
  verified_at_load: true
not_built_by_lane_5:     # PARTITION gap - subsystem J is unassigned
  - hermes-cage-configuration-files
  - background-host-egress-override
  - background-window-stop-unit
  - local-inference-endpoint-service
```

grep -cEi '(api_key|password|secret|token) *:' access/ai-runtime/runtime-contract.yaml access/ai-runtime/vendor-checklist.yaml | grep -c ':0'
grep -c 'not_built_by_lane_5:' access/ai-runtime/runtime-contract.yaml
```

```
RUNTIME CONTRACT OK: 7 capabilities, 6 runtimes, 7 vendor checks
1
6
2
1
```
**STOP** if the printed §35.1 table has a row count other than 7, or the §35.6 table other than 7, or if you are asked to add a seventh approved runtime. Adding or removing a runtime is a configuration change with a recorded decision (§35.2) — an L0 decision, not yours. Open a blocker with `TASK=L5-T24`.

### L5-T25 — No-API-keys check (§36.6, invariant 84)

> **NOTE: SUPERSEDED BY FD-047 — NOT BUILT, NOT PENDING**
> Task body retired per FD-047. Authoritative check in access/ai-toolchain/checks/ per L5-05.

**Phase** P3 · **Size** M · **Mode** LANE · **Deps** L5-T24 · **Spec** §36.6 (spec lines 3232–3238), invariant 84
**Files:** `access/ai-runtime/tools/check_no_api_keys.py`, `access/tests/test_no_api_keys.py`, `access/tests/fixtures/env-clean.txt`, `access/tests/fixtures/env-poisoned.txt` (new)

§36.6 states the check mechanically: *`env | grep -i api_key` must return empty.* There is no exemption to interpret. The Hermes endpoint credential is held in the fifth secrets tier on machine hosts and workstation-scoped on a personal seat; it is never an exported developer-environment variable, so it never appears in this check's input.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$REPO_ROOT"
git checkout integration && git pull --ff-only origin integration
git checkout -b lane/5/p3-t25-no-api-keys
mkdir -p access/tests/fixtures
printf '%s\n' 'PATH=/usr/bin' 'HOME=/home/dev' 'EDITOR=vim' > access/tests/fixtures/env-clean.txt
printf '%s\n' 'PATH=/usr/bin' 'OPENAI_API_KEY=redacted-fixture-value' 'HOME=/home/dev' > access/tests/fixtures/env-poisoned.txt
```

Author `access/ai-runtime/tools/check_no_api_keys.py` to this contract, exactly:

- `--env-dump <path>` reads a captured `env` listing (one `NAME=VALUE` per line). It never runs `env` against the machine it is on and never reads the live environment.
- `--profiles <path>...` additionally scans shell profiles and repository `.env` files for an assignment whose **name** matches `(?i)api_key`.
- A match prints `NO-API-KEYS FAIL: <source>: <NAME>` — the **name only, never the value** — once per match, and exits `1`.
- **Fail-closed:** an absent or unreadable input exits `1` with `NO-API-KEYS FAIL: unreadable input <path>`. It never exits `0` by default.
- `--selftest` runs the clean fixture (expects `0`) and the poisoned fixture (expects `1`), then prints `L5-T25 SELF-VERIFY PASS`.

Author `access/tests/test_no_api_keys.py` with exactly **6 tests**: clean env passes; poisoned env fails; the failure names the variable; the failure never prints the value; an absent input fails closed; a profile file containing `API_KEY=` fails.

```bash

"$L5PY" access/ai-runtime/tools/check_no_api_keys.py --selftest
"$L5PY" -m pytest access/tests/test_no_api_keys.py -q
git add access/ai-runtime/tools/check_no_api_keys.py access/tests/test_no_api_keys.py access/tests/fixtures/env-clean.txt access/tests/fixtures/env-poisoned.txt
git commit -m "L5-T25: no-API-keys check (spec 36.6, invariant 84)"
git push -u origin lane/5/p3-t25-no-api-keys
```
